# APTIS Listening

### **Part 1: Information Recognition - Questions 1–13**

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set001-q001 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"numbers_money_codes","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"0"} -->
> **1. A person calls a friend about his new car. How much does the small car cost him?**
>
> <u>A. 3250 pounds</u>
>
> B. 3550 pounds
>
> C. 4250 pounds
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set001-q001-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 0 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Man: I finally bought the small car I told you about! Friend: That's great news! How much did it set you back? Man: The dealer first showed me a larger model for 3,550 pounds, and the version with a full service package came to 4,250. The small one itself cost me 3,250 pounds, which was within my budget. Friend: That sounds like a good deal. Man: Yes, I chose the basic package and used the money I saved to pay for insurance.

<!-- word_comment_id: 0; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 1. A person calls a friend about his new car. How much does the small car cost him? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set001-q002 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"time_date_duration","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"1"} -->
> **2. Two people are talking about meeting for dinner. What time does Ahmed meet Rose?**
>
> A. half past seven
>
> B. quarter past seven
>
> <u>C. quarter to eight</u>
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set001-q002-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 1 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Ahmed: Rose, are we still meeting for dinner tonight? I originally suggested half past seven, but my meeting may not finish until then. past seven is definitely too early for me. Could we make it to eight instead? Rose: That works well. I'll book the table and wait inside if I arrive before you. Just message me when you leave the office. Nobody else needed to change their plans after the updated time was agreed.

<!-- word_comment_id: 1; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 2. Two people are talking about meeting for dinner. What time does Ahmed meet Rose? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set001-q003 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"numbers_money_codes","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"2"} -->
> **3. A man calls the teleshop. What is the teleshop number?**
>
> A. 102030
>
> <u>B. 201030</u>
>
> C. 301020
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set001-q003-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 2 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Man: I'm trying to call the teleshop, but I have three similar numbers written down. Is it 102030? Friend: No, that belongs to the delivery company. And 301020 is the repair line. The teleshop number is 201030. Man: Great, I'll save it properly this time. I need to ask whether the television I ordered is still available. A second check confirmed that no part of the total had been counted twice.

<!-- word_comment_id: 2; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 3. A man calls the teleshop. What is the teleshop number? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set001-q004 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"objects_descriptions","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"3"} -->
> **4. A man is talking to a shopping assistant. What color top is he going to buy?**
>
> A. Green
>
> B. Blue
>
> <u>C. Black</u>
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set001-q004-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 3 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Man: I like the green top in the window, but it is a little too bright for work. Do you have the same design in blue? Assistant: We do, although the blue one is only available in a larger size. Man: In that case, I'll take the black top. It fits well and will match my trousers. I may come back for something more colourful another day.

<!-- word_comment_id: 3; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 4. A man is talking to a shopping assistant. What color top is he going to buy? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set001-q005 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"places_directions","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"4"} -->
> **5. A man is calling his sister. Where are they going to meet?**
>
> A. At the university
>
> B. At the station
>
> <u>C. At the park</u>
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set001-q005-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 4 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Man: Shall we meet at the station before lunch? Sister: It will be extremely crowded because several trains arrive together. The university is , but it is too far from the shops we need. Man: Let's meet at the park instead, beside the main gate. Sister: Perfect. I'll bring the documents, and afterwards we can walk into town together. The nearby landmarks were useful, although neither was the destination itself.

<!-- word_comment_id: 4; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 5. A man is calling his sister. Where are they going to meet? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set001-q006 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"people_relationships","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"5"} -->
> **6. A woman is talking about her vacation. What is the relationship between the speaker and Lisa?**
>
> <u>A. Best friends</u>
>
> B. Mother and daughter
>
> C. Teacher and student
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set001-q006-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 5 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Woman: Lisa and I have just returned from a week by the sea. People sometimes think we are mother and daughter because she is a little older and always looks after me. Others assume she was once my teacher. In fact, we are best friends and have known each other since secondary school. We travel together whenever our work schedules allow it. The explanation helped listeners understand why the other guesses sounded believable.

<!-- word_comment_id: 5; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 6. A woman is talking about her vacation. What is the relationship between the speaker and Lisa? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set001-q007 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"activities_events_details","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"6"} -->
> **7. A woman is talking about her house. What is she going to change in their house?**
>
> <u>A. The window</u>
>
> B. The car
>
> C. The computer
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set001-q007-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 6 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Woman: We talked about replacing the computer because it is slow, and my husband also wants a smaller car. However, the change we are making to the house is the window. The current one is too large and lets in too much sunlight during the afternoon. We have ordered a smaller design with thicker glass, and the work should begin next month. Nothing else needed to change once the main decision had been made.

<!-- word_comment_id: 6; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 7. A woman is talking about her house. What is she going to change in their house? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set001-q008 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"numbers_money_codes","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"7"} -->
> **8. A receptionist is checking the client list of a clinic. How many clients are Americans?**
>
> <u>A. One</u>
>
> B. Two
>
> C. Three
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set001-q008-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 7 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Receptionist: I've checked today's clinic list again. Carl, Harry and Matthew are from the UK, while two other clients are visiting from France. Leslie is the only person from the United States, so we have one American client today. Her appointment is at eleven. Please make sure the correct form is waiting at reception before she arrives. The receipt and the current price list were checked once more afterwards.

<!-- word_comment_id: 7; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 8. A receptionist is checking the client list of a clinic. How many clients are Americans? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set001-q009 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"places_directions","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"8"} -->
> **9. Listen to Anna talking about her routine. Where does Anna go for a walk every morning?**
>
> A. park
>
> B. neighborhood
>
> <u>C. college</u>
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set001-q009-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 8 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Anna: I enjoy walking early because everywhere is before classes begin. I sometimes walk through the park at weekends, and my neighbourhood is pleasant in the evening. Every morning, though, I go for a walk around the college campus. I usually pass the library and sports centre before returning to my accommodation to collect my books for the first lesson. The route becomes simple once the correct building or entrance comes into view.

<!-- word_comment_id: 8; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 9. Listen to Anna talking about her routine. Where does Anna go for a walk every morning? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set001-q010 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"places_directions","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"9"} -->
> **10. Listen to a girl calling the cafe. What did she forget, and where did she leave it?**
>
> A. on the counter
>
> <u>B. in the corner</u>
>
> C. near the door
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set001-q010-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 9 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Girl: Hi, I was in your café this afternoon and I think I left my bag there. I paid at the counter, but I am sure I still had it afterwards. I did not sit near the door. My table was in the corner beside a tall plant, so could you look there first? It is a small brown bag with my keys inside. They planned to arrive early enough to avoid any further confusion there.

<!-- word_comment_id: 9; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 10. Listen to a girl calling the cafe. What did she forget, and where did she leave it? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set001-q011 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"places_directions","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"10"} -->
> **11. Helen is calling a friend about the place her whole family is standing while seeing her off to college. Where are they standing?**
>
> A. library complex
>
> B. university area
>
> <u>C. Residential area</u>
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set001-q011-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 10 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Helen: My whole family has come to see me off before I start college. We are not inside the university area yet, and we are nowhere near the library complex. We are standing in the residential area just outside the campus, beside some small shops and trees. Dad is unloading my bags while everyone else is saying goodbye. It feels exciting and a little emotional.

<!-- word_comment_id: 10; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 11. Helen is calling a friend about the place her whole family is standing while seeing her off to college. Where are they standing? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set001-q012 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"time_date_duration","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"11"} -->
> **12. The woman is calling a friend about meeting for dinner. How long does it take to get to the station?**
>
> A. 30 minutes
>
> <u>B. 40 minutes</u>
>
> C. 50 minutes
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set001-q012-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 11 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Woman: I'm checking the timing for dinner tonight because I need to catch a train afterwards. The journey to the station is not thirty minutes unless I take a taxi, and fifty minutes would include waiting for the bus. Walking normally takes about forty minutes. I'll leave the restaurant early enough to buy my ticket and find the correct platform without rushing. They noted the change immediately so nobody would follow the earlier timetable.

<!-- word_comment_id: 11; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 12. The woman is calling a friend about meeting for dinner. How long does it take to get to the station? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set001-q013 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"time_date_duration","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"12"} -->
> **13. Listen to a man talking about their train journey. What time did the train depart?**
>
> A. 9:00
>
> <u>B. 9:30</u>
>
> C. 10:00
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set001-q013-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 12 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Man: Our train was supposed to depart at nine, but a signal problem caused a delay. At first, staff warned us that we might have to wait until ten o'clock. Fortunately, the problem was fixed sooner, and the train actually left at half past nine. We arrived later than planned, but we still had enough time to attend the morning meeting. The remaining activities could continue normally once the correct time was clear.

<!-- word_comment_id: 12; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 13. Listen to a man talking about their train journey. What time did the train depart? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set002-q001 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"numbers_money_codes","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"13"} -->
> **14. Listen to a woman asking about a flight. How much does the flight in the morning cost?**
>
> A. 300 pounds
>
> <u>B. 350 pounds</u>
>
> C. 400 pounds
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set002-q001-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 13 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Woman: I'm comparing the flights for my trip next month. There is an afternoon service for 300 pounds, but it arrives too late for the conference. The flexible ticket costs 400 pounds because changes are included. The morning flight I need is 350 pounds. It is not the cheapest option, although its arrival time is much more convenient. The figures became clear once the different products or categories were separated.

<!-- word_comment_id: 13; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 14. Listen to a woman asking about a flight. How much does the flight in the morning cost? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set002-q002 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"numbers_money_codes","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"14"} -->
> **15. Listen to a friend talking about selling her music player. How much did she sell it for?**
>
> <u>A. 50 dollars</u>
>
> B. 60 dollars
>
> C. 40 dollars
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set002-q002-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 14 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Woman: I advertised my old music player for sixty dollars, but nobody wanted to pay that much. One buyer offered forty, which felt too low because it still worked perfectly. We eventually agreed on fifty dollars, and I sold it last weekend. I used part of the money to buy new headphones and put the rest into my savings account. Everyone involved used the latest record instead of relying on memory.

<!-- word_comment_id: 14; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 15. Listen to a friend talking about selling her music player. How much did she sell it for? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set002-q003 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"reasons_opinions_purpose","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"15"} -->
> **16. Listen to a woman explaining why he was late. What is the main reason he gets late?**
>
> A. Overslept
>
> B. Forgot something
>
> <u>C. Missed the train</u>
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set002-q003-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 15 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Woman: He is often late, so people assume he oversleeps or returns home because he has forgotten something. Today neither of those things happened. He left the house on time but reached the platform just as the doors closed, so the main reason was that he missed the train. He had to wait twenty minutes for the next service and called the office immediately. Once that was understood, the rest of the situation made much more sense.

<!-- word_comment_id: 15; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 16. Listen to a woman explaining why he was late. What is the main reason he gets late? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set002-q004 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"numbers_money_codes","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"16"} -->
> **17. A mom is calling her son to remind him about picking up groceries. How much is an egg?**
>
> <u>A. £1.50 (One pounds fifty)</u>
>
> B. £2.50 (Two pounds fifty)
>
> C. £3.50 (Three pounds fifty)
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set002-q004-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 16 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Mother: Hi, could you pick up a few groceries on your way home? Son: Sure, Mum. What do you need? Mother: The milk is two pounds fifty, and the large loaf of bread costs three pounds fifty. A dozen eggs is only one pound fifty today, so please buy two boxes if they still have them. Son: Got it. Is that everything? Mother: Yes, I need the eggs for a cake, but the other things are for tomorrow's breakfast. Thank you, dear.

<!-- word_comment_id: 16; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 17. A mom is calling her son to remind him about picking up groceries. How much is an egg? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set002-q005 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"places_directions","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"17"} -->
> **18. A man is calling his wife. Where will they meet?**
>
> A. At home
>
> B. In the garden
>
> <u>C. Outside the shop</u>
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set002-q005-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 17 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Man: Hi, I'm calling to confirm where we should meet. I could wait at home, but then we might miss each other when you arrive. Woman: True, and the garden is completely wet after all that rain. Man: Exactly. Let's meet outside the shop opposite the station instead. I'll be standing near the entrance with a blue umbrella. Woman: Perfect, I'll head there now. We can walk home together from there.

<!-- word_comment_id: 17; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 18. A man is calling his wife. Where will they meet? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set002-q006 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"time_date_duration","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"18"} -->
> **19. Julie is asking her professor about the assignment. When is the work due?**
>
> A. On Thursday morning
>
> B. On Friday morning
>
> <u>C. On Saturday morning</u>
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set002-q006-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 18 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Julie: Professor, I cannot finish the assignment by Thursday morning. Could I submit it on Friday? Professor: Friday is difficult because the department will be closed. I can extend the deadline until Saturday morning. Upload it before midday, and make sure the reference list is included. Julie: Thank you. That gives me an extra evening to check the final section carefully. The new arrangement also avoided a rushed journey at the busiest part of the day.

<!-- word_comment_id: 18; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 19. Julie is asking her professor about the assignment. When is the work due? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set002-q007 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"numbers_money_codes","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"19"} -->
> **20. A tour guide is introducing a tourist destination. How many people live in the town?**
>
> A. 8,000
>
> B. 9,000
>
> <u>C. 10,000</u>
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set002-q007-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 19 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Tour guide: Welcome to our town. Older guidebooks say around eight thousand people lived here, and an estimate from several years ago suggested nine thousand. The latest local records show that the population has reached ten thousand. Most new residents live near the railway station, while the historic centre still contains the small shops and cafés we will visit today. The written information was updated immediately to prevent another mistake later.

<!-- word_comment_id: 19; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 20. A tour guide is introducing a tourist destination. How many people live in the town? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set002-q008 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"activities_events_details","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"20"} -->
> **21. A man is talking about his routine after work. What is the man going to do after work?**
>
> <u>A. Goes running</u>
>
> B. Cycles home
>
> C. Meets his client
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set002-q008-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 20 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Man: After work, I do not usually cycle home because the main road is too busy. I sometimes meet a client in the late afternoon, but there is no appointment today. I'm going running in the park instead. It helps me clear my mind after sitting at a desk, and I normally finish with a short walk before going home for dinner. The other possibilities were useful to discuss but were not selected.

<!-- word_comment_id: 20; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 21. A man is talking about his routine after work. What is the man going to do after work? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set002-q009 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"activities_events_details","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"21"} -->
> **22. Two friends are talking about their favorite activities. What is the woman's favorite form of entertainment?**
>
> A. Reading books
>
> <u>B. Going to the theatre</u>
>
> C. Playing chess with her cousin
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set002-q009-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 21 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Man: Do you spend most of your free time reading? Woman: I enjoy books, and I occasionally play chess with my cousin, but neither is my favourite form of entertainment. I most enjoy going to the theatre. Seeing actors perform live feels more exciting than watching something at home, and I usually discuss the play with friends over coffee afterwards. The most important feature appeared before the speaker moved on to other information.

<!-- word_comment_id: 21; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 22. Two friends are talking about their favorite activities. What is the woman's favorite form of entertainment? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set002-q010 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"time_date_duration","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"22"} -->
> **23. A man is calling his friend, Maria. When will he see her?**
>
> <u>A. at 9 am on Sunday</u>
>
> B. at 10 am on Saturday
>
> C. at 8 am on Sunday
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set002-q010-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 22 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Man: Hi, Maria. I'll be in your town this weekend! Maria: Wonderful! When should we meet? Man: I first thought I could visit at ten on Saturday, but my train does not arrive until the evening. Eight on Sunday would be a little early after the journey. Maria: No rush - what time works for you? Man: I'll see you at nine o'clock on Sunday. We can have breakfast together and then walk around the old market. Maria: That sounds perfect, I'll look forward to it.

<!-- word_comment_id: 22; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 23. A man is calling his friend, Maria. When will he see her? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set002-q011 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"time_date_duration","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"23"} -->
> **24. A man is calling his colleague about a meeting with clients. When will the meeting start?**
>
> A. at 9.15
>
> <u>B. at 10.15</u>
>
> C. at 11.15
>

<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 23 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">

Man: I'm calling about tomorrow's client meeting. The room is available from 9:15, but we need time to set up the equipment. Eleven fifteen is when the clients must leave for another appointment. We will therefore start at 10:15 as originally agreed. Please arrive twenty minutes early so we can test the presentation and organise the printed documents. The revised timing left enough room for travel and preparation beforehand.

<!-- word_comment_id: 23; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 24. A man is calling his colleague about a meeting with clients. When will the meeting start? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set002-q012 -->

> **25. A customer is calling the hotline of a department store. Which number to press in order to buy a computer?**
>
> A. One
>
> B. Two
>
> <u>C. Three</u>
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set002-q012-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 24 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Recorded message: Thank you for calling the department store. Press one if you want to make a complaint about a recent purchase. Press two for repairs or technical support. If you are interested in buying a new computer, press three to speak to a sales adviser. Please have an idea of your budget and the type of work you need the computer to handle. No extra charge or item needed to be added to that figure.

<!-- word_comment_id: 24; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 25. A customer is calling the hotline of a department store. Which number to press in order to buy a computer? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set002-q013 -->

> **26. An expert is talking about the lack of satisfaction at work. What should be the solution?**
>
> A. Raise the salary
>
> B. Seek a new job
>
> <u>C. Request a transfer</u>
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set002-q013-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 25 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Expert: A higher salary may make an unhappy employee feel better for a short time, and searching for a completely new job is another possibility. However, if the main problem is the local team or working environment, the best solution may be to request a transfer to another branch. The employee can keep the same benefits while trying a different manager and group of colleagues.

<!-- word_comment_id: 25; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 26. An expert is talking about the lack of satisfaction at work. What should be the solution? -->

</details>

The final comments added context without changing the main description.

<!-- word_comment_id: 30; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 31. An artist is talking about her job. What is the difference in her job? -->

The choice fitted the situation better than the other ideas they had discussed.

>

Finance expert: Young people should certainly avoid spending too much on clothes, and they should save something for emergencies whenever possible. Learning to manage a limited amount is an important financial skill. That option matched the person's needs more closely than the alternatives.

<!-- word_comment_id: 37; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 38. A finance expert is giving advice to young people. What shouldn't they do? -->

You can visit it until six if you need tickets, maps or help with tomorrow's activities. A clear landmark nearby makes the correct place easy to recognise on arrival.

The other possibilities were useful to discuss but were not selected.

Kay: Oh, it was no trouble at all! How did it go? Douglas: Your advice solved the problem with our final section, and the client was very pleased. Kay: That's such a relief to hear! Douglas: When things are less busy, I'd like to buy you coffee and tell you the full story. Kay: I'd love that. Well done to you both!

>
> A. Buy in bulk
>
> <u>B. Use the public transport</u>
>
> C. Put money into the bank
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set005-q013-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 64 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Woman: Buying in bulk can reduce the price of some products, and putting money into a bank account is useful for long-term savings. My most practical daily advice is to use public transport instead of driving. Bus or train fares are usually lower than petrol, parking and car maintenance. Try it for a month and keep a record of how much you save. It also left enough time and energy for the rest of the day.

<!-- word_comment_id: 64; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 65. Listen to a woman giving advice on saving money. What advice does she give to save money? -->

</details>

The final record was saved so the same confusion would not happen again.

>

>

>

Have you talked to them about it? Man: I have tried to explain the career benefits and promised to visit regularly, but they still want me to stay close to home.

We clearly agree on that, even if our opinions differ about almost everything else. The most important feature appeared before the speaker moved on to other information.

<!-- word_comment_id: 69; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 70. Two friends are discussing a movie they recently watched. What elements of the film do they agree on? -->

> **71. Doctor's office is calling to change the appointment. When is the new appointment?**
>
> A. On Friday 14th
>
> B. On Thursday 30th
>
> <u>C. On Thursday 13th</u>
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set006-q006-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 70 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Clinic message: This is the doctor's office calling about your appointment. Friday the 14th is no longer available because the clinic will be closed, and Thursday the 30th belongs to another patient's booking. Your new appointment is on Thursday the 13th at 10:30. Please call us if that date is impossible, and bring your previous test results when you come. The updated schedule suited the people involved better than either earlier suggestion.

<!-- word_comment_id: 70; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 71. Doctor's office is calling to change the appointment. When is the new appointment? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set006-q007 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"numbers_money_codes","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"71"} -->
> **72. Max is calling his friend. How much can Max pay for the computer?**
>
> A. 150 pounds
>
> <u>B. 250 pounds</u>
>
> C. 350 pounds
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set006-q007-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 71 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Max: Hi, I'm calling about the computer you are selling. Is it still available? Friend: Yes, it is! What offer were you thinking? Max: I could offer 150 pounds for an older machine, but 350 is beyond my budget. I can pay 250 pounds for yours if the keyboard, screen and charger are all working. Friend: They are all in good condition, I promise. Max: Great. I would also like to test it briefly before I decide. Friend: Of course, no problem. Max: If everything is fine, I can collect it on Saturday morning. Friend: Saturday works perfectly. See you then.

<!-- word_comment_id: 71; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 72. Max is calling his friend. How much can Max pay for the computer? -->

</details>

It sounds refreshing in this warm weather. Please serve it without too much sugar, and could I also have a glass of water on the side? It also left enough time and energy for the rest of the day.

The practical details made that option more suitable than either earlier suggestion.

<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Student: Germany was our original choice, and Spain was discussed because the weather would be warmer. Next semester, we will study in France. The university has a strong programme for international students, and several of our courses will be taught in English. I have started learning basic French so everyday tasks will be easier when we arrive. The choice fitted the situation better than the other ideas they had discussed.

<!-- word_comment_id: 119; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 120. What country will they study next semester? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set010-q004 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"activities_events_details","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"120"} -->
> **121. What does he advise young people to do to save money?**
>
> A. Use public transportation
>
> B. Buy second-hand items
>
> <u>C. Cook for yourself</u>
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set010-q004-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 120 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Financial adviser: Using public transport and buying second-hand items can both reduce costs. My strongest advice to young people is to cook for yourself. Meals prepared at home are usually much cheaper than eating in cafés or ordering food. Plan several simple dishes, buy only what you need and take lunch to work or college instead of purchasing it every day. It also left enough time and energy for the rest of the day.

<!-- word_comment_id: 120; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 121. What does he advise young people to do to save money? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set010-q005 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"reasons_opinions_purpose","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"121"} -->
> **122. Listen to a woman explaining why she was late. What is the main reason she gets late?**
>
> A. Car broke down
>
> B. Heavy traffic
>
> <u>C. Missed the train</u>
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set010-q005-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 121 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Woman: My car did not break down, and although the traffic was heavy, I still reached the station in time. The problem was that I went to the wrong platform and missed the train. I had to wait for the next service, which made me late for work. Now I check the departure board carefully as soon as I enter the station. That detail changed how everyone involved understood the problem.

<!-- word_comment_id: 121; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 122. Listen to a woman explaining why she was late. What is the main reason she gets late? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set010-q006 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"activities_events_details","classification_status":"review_required","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"122"} -->

>
> <u>A. A surprise performance</u>
>
> B. A speech
>
> C. A firework display
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set010-q006-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 122 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Girl: The show includes a short speech near the beginning, and there will be a firework display outside before the interval. It will end with a surprise performance. The organisers have not announced the performer, but people expect it to be someone well known. I'm staying until the final curtain because I do not want to miss the part everyone is talking about. The practical details made that option more suitable than either earlier suggestion.

<!-- word_comment_id: 122; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 123. A girl is talking about a show she will attend. What will it end with? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set010-q007 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"activities_events_details","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"123"} -->
> **124. A woman is chatting with a friend about her afternoon plans. What is she going to do this afternoon?**
>
> A. Sleep at home
>
> B. Go to the cinema
>
> <u>C. Go on a city tour</u>
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set010-q007-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 123 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Woman: I could sleep at home this afternoon, and the cinema has a film I would like to see. Instead, I'm going on a city tour. I have lived here for two years but still know very little about the old buildings. The tour starts at the main square and finishes beside the river, so I may have dinner there afterwards. After making the decision, the remaining arrangements were straightforward.

<!-- word_comment_id: 123; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 124. A woman is chatting with a friend about her afternoon plans. What is she going to do this afternoon? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set010-q008 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"numbers_money_codes","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"124"} -->
> **125. The woman is discussing her new exercise routine. How much time does she spend cycling?**
>
> A. 45 minutes
>
> <u>B. 35 minutes</u>
>
> C. 60 minutes
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set010-q008-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 124 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Woman: I started my new exercise routine by planning forty-five minutes of cycling, but that left me too tired. An hour would include travelling to the gym and warming up. I actually spend thirty-five minutes cycling. After that, I stretch for ten minutes and walk home slowly. The shorter session is easier to repeat several times each week without losing motivation. That amount matched the official record rather than either earlier estimate.

<!-- word_comment_id: 124; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 125. The woman is discussing her new exercise routine. How much time does she spend cycling? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set010-q009 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"time_date_duration","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"125"} -->
> **126. A woman is calling her son. What time will the mother meet the son?**
>
> A. Six o’clock
>
> B. Two o’clock
>
> <u>C. Three o'clock</u>
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set010-q009-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 125 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Mother: Hi, I'm calling about when we should meet today. I originally said I would meet you at two, but my appointment will not finish in time. Son: That's fine. What time works instead? Mother: Six o'clock is possible, although it would be too late for the shopping we need to do. I'll meet you at three o'clock outside the station. Son: Alright, I'll wait near the ticket machines. Mother: Call me if the train is delayed or you arrive before I do.

<!-- word_comment_id: 125; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 126. A woman is calling her son. What time will the mother meet the son? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set010-q010 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"objects_descriptions","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"126"} -->
> **127. Jack is calling to invite a friend to his house. What color is Jack’s house??**
>
> A. Black
>
> <u>B. Red</u>
>
> C. Green
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set010-q010-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 126 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Jack: I'm calling to give you directions to my place. Friend: Great, which house is yours? Jack: When you come, do not stop at the black one on the corner. The green house belongs to my neighbour. Mine is the red house halfway along the street, with a small tree beside the gate. Friend: Got it, the red one with the tree. Jack: Exactly! I'll leave the outside light on if it gets dark, and you can park in the space directly in front.

<!-- word_comment_id: 126; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 127. Jack is calling to invite a friend to his house. What color is Jack’s house?? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set010-q011 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"activities_events_details","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"127"} -->
> **128. A man is talking about his daily routine. What does he do after work?**
>
> A. Go home
>
> B. Go to the coffee shop
>
> <u>C. Play football</u>
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set010-q011-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 127 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Man: After work, I do not go straight home unless the weather is terrible. I sometimes stop at the coffee shop, but only when I am meeting someone. Most evenings, I play football with colleagues at the sports centre. The match helps me forget about work, and afterwards I walk home, take a shower and eat a late dinner. The choice fitted the situation better than the other ideas they had discussed.

<!-- word_comment_id: 127; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 128. A man is talking about his daily routine. What does he do after work? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set010-q012 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"time_date_duration","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"128"} -->
> **129. An author is talking about her daily routine. When does she usually write?**
>
> <u>A. In the afternoons</u>
>
> B. In the mornings
>
> C. At night
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set010-q012-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 128 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Author: I have tried writing in the morning, but I spend too long answering messages and organising the day. At night, I am usually too tired to concentrate. I normally write in the afternoons, after lunch and before the house becomes busy again. I work for about three hours, then stop even if a chapter is not completely finished. Everyone agreed to confirm the smaller details by message later that day.

<!-- word_comment_id: 128; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 129. An author is talking about her daily routine. When does she usually write? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set010-q013 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"activities_events_details","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"129"} -->
> **130. A woman is talking about her usual Saturday routine. What does she usually do on Saturdays?**
>
> A. Goes shopping
>
> <u>B. Sees her family</u>
>
> C. Goes to a birthday party
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set010-q013-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 129 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Woman: I occasionally go shopping on Saturday, and last month I attended a friend's birthday party. My usual Saturday routine is to see my family. We have lunch at my parents' house and often stay until the evening. It is the one day when everyone is normally free, so we try not to arrange other activities unless something important comes up. The alternatives remained possible on another occasion, but not this time.

<!-- word_comment_id: 129; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 130. A woman is talking about her usual Saturday routine. What does she usually do on Saturdays? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set011-q001 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"activities_events_details","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"130"} -->
> **131. A man is talking about his family trip. What does the man’s wife enjoy?**
>
> <u>A. Photography</u>
>
> B. Shopping
>
> C. Hiking
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set011-q001-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 130 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Man: On family trips, I enjoy hiking and the children are always interested in shopping for souvenirs. My wife most enjoys photography. She carries a small camera everywhere and stops whenever she sees an unusual building, landscape or local market. At the end of the trip, she creates an album, which is much more interesting than the photographs I take on my phone. The practical details made that option more suitable than either earlier suggestion.

<!-- word_comment_id: 130; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 131. A man is talking about his family trip. What does the man’s wife enjoy? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set011-q002 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"reasons_opinions_purpose","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"131"} -->
> **132. A woman is talking about her job. What encouraged her to become a scientist?**
>
> A. Her computer
>
> B. Her mother
>
> <u>C. A large stone</u>
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set011-q002-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 131 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Woman: My mother supported my education, and I used a computer for many school projects, but neither first inspired me to become a scientist. It began when I found a large stone near my home. I wanted to know why it contained different colours and where it came from. That curiosity led me to read about geology and eventually choose science as a career. Understanding the real cause made it easier to decide what to do next.

<!-- word_comment_id: 131; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 132. A woman is talking about her job. What encouraged her to become a scientist? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set011-q003 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"time_date_duration","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"132"} -->
> **133. Listen to the speaker talking about their weekly schedule. When is the meeting scheduled?**
>
> A. Monday morning
>
> B. Friday afternoon
>
> <u>C. Wednesday afternoon</u>
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set011-q003-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 132 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Speaker: Our team is unavailable on Monday morning because the weekly reports are due. Friday afternoon was suggested, but several people will already have left for a conference. The meeting is scheduled for Wednesday afternoon at two. We will use the main conference room and should finish before four. Please send any agenda items to me by Tuesday evening. The remaining activities could continue normally once the correct time was clear.

<!-- word_comment_id: 132; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 133. Listen to the speaker talking about their weekly schedule. When is the meeting scheduled? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set011-q004 -->

>
> A. 1500
>
> <u>B. 2000</u>
>
> C. 2500
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set011-q004-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 133 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Newsreader: The first version of the housing plan proposed 1,500 homes, while campaigners feared the number might rise to 2,500. The approved development will contain 2,000 new houses. Building will take place in several stages, and the project also includes a school, shops and public green space. The first residents are expected to move in within three years. The figures became clear once the different products or categories were separated.

<!-- word_comment_id: 133; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 134. A man is reading the news about a housing development plan. How many new houses are going to be built? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set011-q005 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"activities_events_details","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"134"} -->

>
> A. Writer
>
> B. Librarian
>
> <u>C. Teacher</u>
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set011-q005-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 134 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Writer: People know me as a writer now, and I worked in a library for a short period while finishing my first book. My first job was as a teacher. I taught young children for five years. That experience helped me explain ideas clearly, and many of the characters in my early stories were inspired by situations I observed in the classroom. Nothing else needed to change once the main decision had been made.

<!-- word_comment_id: 134; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 135. Listen to a writer talking about her job. What is her first job? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set011-q006 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"activities_events_details","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"135"} -->
> **136. Listen to Marry talking to Jane while waiting for James. What did they decide to do?**
>
> A. Cancel the meeting
>
> <u>B. Having the meeting without him</u>
>
> C. Wait for him longer
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set011-q006-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 135 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Mary: James is already twenty minutes late. Should we cancel the meeting? Jane: We need to make a decision today, and waiting longer will leave us no time. Let's have the meeting without him and send him the notes afterwards. Mary: Agreed. We can begin with the budget, and if he arrives before we finish, he can join the discussion. The choice fitted the situation better than the other ideas they had discussed.

<!-- word_comment_id: 135; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 136. Listen to Marry talking to Jane while waiting for James. What did they decide to do? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set011-q007 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"reasons_opinions_purpose","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"136"} -->
> **137. Vincent is calling James. Why does Vincent call James?**
>
> A. Invite him to a party
>
> <u>B. Suggest a drink</u>
>
> C. Ask for help
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set011-q007-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 136 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Vincent: Hi, James. I'm not calling to invite you to a party, and I do not need help with work. I wondered whether you would like to go for a drink this evening. There is a new café near the station that stays open late. We could meet there after six and catch up, since we have not spoken properly for several weeks. The other explanations sounded possible at first but did not match the evidence.

<!-- word_comment_id: 136; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 137. Vincent is calling James. Why does Vincent call James? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set011-q008 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"time_date_duration","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"137"} -->
> **138. Samia is going to meet her friend. What time are they going to meet?**
>
> A. 9:30
>
> B. 11:00
>
> <u>C. 10:00</u>
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set011-q008-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 137 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Samia: Could we meet at 9:30? Friend: I have an appointment that does not finish until then. Eleven would be possible, but it is later than we need. Samia: Let's meet at ten o'clock outside the library. Friend: Perfect. I'll return my books first, and afterwards we can walk to the market and have something to eat. Nobody else needed to change their plans after the updated time was agreed.

<!-- word_comment_id: 137; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 138. Samia is going to meet her friend. What time are they going to meet? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set011-q009 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"activities_events_details","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"138"} -->
> **139. Anna is calling her brother Max. What does Anna do later in the afternoon?**
>
> A. Pick up the kids
>
> <u>B. Stay late at the office</u>
>
> C. Hang out with friends
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set011-q009-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 138 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Anna: Max, could you pick up the children this afternoon? I cannot do it because I have to stay late at the office. Max: Of course, is everything alright? Anna: An urgent report is due today, so I have to stay. I originally planned to leave on time and meet friends afterwards. Max: Don't worry, I'll pick them up.

<!-- word_comment_id: 138; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 139. Anna is calling her brother Max. What does Anna do later in the afternoon? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set011-q010 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"activities_events_details","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"139"} -->

>
> <u>A. Action film</u>
>
> B. Romantic film
>
> C. Comedy film
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set011-q010-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 139 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Radio presenter: I watched three new films this week. The romantic film has good performances, and the comedy is enjoyable but rather predictable. The film I recommend is the action film. Its story moves , the main character is convincing and the final sequence is genuinely exciting. It is best seen at the cinema because the sound and large screen add a great deal. The full description explained why the earlier possibilities had sounded convincing.

<!-- word_comment_id: 139; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 140. A woman is talking about her favorite film on the radio. What film did she recommend? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set011-q011 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"travel_transport_mode","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"140"} -->
> **141. Greg is talking about a working day in his life. How does he go to work?**
>
> <u>A. By bus</u>
>
> B. By car
>
> C. By bike
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set011-q011-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 140 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Greg: I own a car, but city-centre parking is too expensive. Cycling would be healthy, although the main road feels unsafe during rush hour. I go to work by bus. The stop is close to my house, and the journey takes about thirty minutes. I usually use the time to read the news or prepare a list of tasks for the day. The other possibilities were useful to discuss but were not selected.

<!-- word_comment_id: 140; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 141. Greg is talking about a working day in his life. How does he go to work? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set011-q012 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"time_date_duration","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"141"} -->
> **142. Listen to a nutrition expert. What time is the best for children to eat fruit?**
>
> <u>A. In the morning</u>
>
> B. In the afternoon
>
> C. In the evening
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set011-q012-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 141 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Nutrition expert: Fruit is healthy at any time, but for children the best time is in the morning. It provides energy and can be included with breakfast or a mid-morning snack. In the afternoon, many children already have other snacks, and fruit late in the evening may reduce their appetite for dinner. Offer a small variety rather than forcing them to eat a large amount.

<!-- word_comment_id: 141; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 142. Listen to a nutrition expert. What time is the best for children to eat fruit? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set011-q013 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"reasons_opinions_purpose","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"142"} -->
> **143. Listen to a woman explaining her morning routine to her friend. Why do women get up early?**
>
> A. To go to work
>
> B. To take care of their kids
>
> <u>C. To have some quiet time</u>
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set011-q013-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 142 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Woman: I do not get up early simply to go to work, and the children are usually still asleep at that time. I wake before everyone else to have some time. I make tea, read for a few minutes and plan the day without interruption. Once the family is awake, the house becomes busy, so that peaceful half hour is important to me. The clarification prevented the same misunderstanding from affecting the next decision.

<!-- word_comment_id: 142; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 143. Listen to a woman explaining her morning routine to her friend. Why do women get up early? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set012-q001 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"reasons_opinions_purpose","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"143"} -->
> **144. A man is talking to his friend. Why does he need to learn to drive?**
>
> A. To go on a road trip
>
> B. To take his family on vacations
>
> <u>C. He has to drive to work</u>
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set012-q001-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 143 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Friend: So why are you finally learning to drive? Man: A road trip would be enjoyable, and I hope to take my family on holiday in the future. But the immediate reason I need to learn to drive is that I have to drive to work. My new office is outside the town, and no bus arrives early enough for my shift. Friend: That makes a lot of sense. Man: I have booked lessons and hope to pass the test within a few months. Friend: Good luck with it!

<!-- word_comment_id: 143; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 144. A man is talking to his friend. Why does he need to learn to drive? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set012-q002 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"objects_descriptions","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"144"} -->
> **145. Two friends are talking with each other. What did they both buy?**
>
> <u>A. Clothes</u>
>
> B. Shoes
>
> C. Books
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set012-q002-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 144 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Friend 1: I went shopping yesterday and bought some clothes for the holiday. Friend 2: So did I. I looked at shoes first and nearly bought a novel, but I needed a lighter jacket and two shirts. Friend 1: I bought trousers and a dress. At least we both chose the same type of purchase, although our bags looked completely different when we met afterwards.

<!-- word_comment_id: 144; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 145. Two friends are talking with each other. What did they both buy? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set012-q003 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"numbers_money_codes","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"145"} -->
> **146. Listening to a tour guide talking about Rock City. How old is the city?**
>
> A. 1000 years
>
> B. 2000 years
>
> <u>C. 1500 years</u>
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set012-q003-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 145 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Tour guide: Some local stories claim Rock City is two thousand years old, while older tourist guides give a figure of one thousand. Archaeological evidence suggests the city is about fifteen hundred years old. The earliest settlement was much smaller than the modern town. During today's tour, we will see part of the original wall and several objects discovered beneath the market square. That characteristic remained the most useful way to describe the person or thing.

<!-- word_comment_id: 145; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 146. Listening to a tour guide talking about Rock City. How old is the city? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set012-q004 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"places_directions","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"146"} -->
> **147. Listen to the instructions of a university. Where is the main office?**
>
> A. in the basement
>
> <u>B. on the first floor</u>
>
> C. on the second floor
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set012-q004-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 146 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

University instructions: The basement contains storage rooms, and the student support centre is on the second floor. The main office is on the first floor of the central building. Go through the glass entrance and turn left after reception. Staff there can answer about enrolment, fees and timetables. The office is open from nine until five on weekdays. Signs in the area should guide visitors through the final part of the journey.

<!-- word_comment_id: 146; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 147. Listen to the instructions of a university. Where is the main office? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set012-q005 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"numbers_money_codes","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"147"} -->
> **148. Listen to the director talking about sales of his company. How many copies of Freeze Frame magazine were sold?**
>
> A. Over 200,000 copies
>
> <u>B. Over 300,000 copies</u>
>
> C. Over 500,000 copies
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set012-q005-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 147 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Director: Last month, Freeze Frame sold just over 200,000 copies, and our long-term target is 500,000. This month, sales rose to over 300,000 copies. Most of the increase came from digital subscriptions, although newsagent sales also improved. We are pleased with the result, but we still need to attract younger readers if we want the magazine to continue growing. The written information was updated immediately to prevent another mistake later.

<!-- word_comment_id: 147; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 148. Listen to the director talking about sales of his company. How many copies of Freeze Frame magazine were sold? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set012-q006 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"objects_descriptions","classification_status":"review_required","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"148"} -->

>
> A. Wallet
>
> B. Phone
>
> <u>C. Glasses</u>
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set012-q006-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 148 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Man: I realised after leaving my friend's house that something was missing. My wallet was in my coat, and I was using my phone on the journey home. I had left my glasses at the house, probably on the table beside the sofa. I called immediately, and my friend found them. I'll collect them tomorrow rather than risk damaging my spare pair. The other possibilities were useful to discuss but were not selected.

<!-- word_comment_id: 148; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 149. What did he leave at a friend’s house? / What did the man lose? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set012-q007 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"people_relationships","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"149"} -->
> **150. Listen to the conversation. Who is she taking a photo of?**
>
> <u>A. The girl's team</u>
>
> B. The boys' team
>
> C. The coaches
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set012-q007-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 149 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Man: Are you taking photographs of the boys' team? Woman: No, they have already finished, and the coaches asked not to be photographed during their meeting. I'm taking a photo of the girls' team before their final match. They are standing near the goal now. I want one formal picture and then a few natural shots while they warm up. The explanation helped listeners understand why the other guesses sounded believable.

<!-- word_comment_id: 149; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 150. Listen to the conversation. Who is she taking a photo of? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set012-q008 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"time_date_duration","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"150"} -->
> **151. Listen to David talking about the conference. How long did he talk in the speech?**
>
> A. 10 minutes
>
> <u>B. 15 minutes</u>
>
> C. 20 minutes
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set012-q008-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 150 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

David: The conference organiser gave me twenty minutes in total, but that included the introduction and I had originally prepared only ten minutes of material. My actual speech lasted fifteen minutes. That was enough time to explain the main results without rushing. Afterwards, the audience asked several useful , and two people stayed behind to discuss the project in more detail. The updated schedule suited the people involved better than either earlier suggestion.

<!-- word_comment_id: 150; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 151. Listen to David talking about the conference. How long did he talk in the speech? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set012-q009 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"activities_events_details","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"151"} -->
> **152. A woman is talking about her family’s weekend. What does the family do most weekends?**
>
> <u>A. Go for a walk</u>
>
> B. Go to the beach
>
> C. Visit relatives
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set012-q009-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 151 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Woman: We visit relatives on special occasions, and in summer we sometimes spend a day at the beach. Most weekends, my family goes for a walk. We choose a park or a countryside path and take a picnic if the weather is good. It gives us time to talk without screens or household jobs, and even the children usually enjoy being outdoors. The choice fitted the situation better than the other ideas they had discussed.

<!-- word_comment_id: 151; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 152. A woman is talking about her family’s weekend. What does the family do most weekends? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set012-q010 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"objects_descriptions","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"152"} -->
> **153. Soobin is talking about his favorite room. What is Soobin's favorite room?**
>
> A. Kitchen
>
> B. Living room
>
> <u>C. Bathroom</u>
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set012-q010-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 152 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Soobin: The kitchen is where everyone gathers for meals, and the living room has the biggest television. My favourite room is the bathroom. It may sound unusual, but it is the place in the house. I like having a long shower after work, and nobody interrupts me there. The room also has a large window that brings in plenty of natural light. The other features were noticeable but were less important in the description.

<!-- word_comment_id: 152; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 153. Soobin is talking about his favorite room. What is Soobin's favorite room? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set012-q011 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"activities_events_details","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"153"} -->
> **154. A woman is talking about her weekends. What did she do last week?**
>
> A. Went out with friends
>
> <u>B. Stayed at home</u>
>
> C. Visited a café
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set012-q011-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 153 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Woman: I often go out with friends at weekends, and I occasionally visit a café on Sunday afternoon. Last week, however, I stayed at home. The weather was terrible, so I cooked a large meal, watched two films and finally finished a book I had been reading for months. It was not exciting, but I felt rested by Monday morning. The alternatives remained possible on another occasion, but not this time.

<!-- word_comment_id: 153; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 154. A woman is talking about her weekends. What did she do last week? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set012-q012 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"places_directions","classification_status":"review_required","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"154"} -->
