>
> A. Living room
>
> B. Bedroom
>
> <u>C. Kitchen</u>
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set012-q012-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 154 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Saleswoman: The living room looks very spacious because of its high ceiling, and the main bedroom has enough space for large furniture. However, the kitchen is the largest room in the property. It includes a dining area and doors leading to the garden. The previous owners extended it several years ago, so it is ideal for a family that enjoys cooking and entertaining. Signs in the area should guide visitors through the final part of the journey.

<!-- word_comment_id: 154; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 155. Listen to a saleswoman talking about a property. Which room is the largest? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set012-q013 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"objects_descriptions","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"155"} -->
> **156. Two friends are talking about their trip. What will the weather be like?**
>
> <u>A. Cold and wet</u>
>
> B. Hot and sunny
>
> C. Mild and dry
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set012-q013-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 155 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Friend 1: The forecast has changed again. I hoped the trip would be hot and sunny, and yesterday they predicted mild, dry weather. Friend 2: Unfortunately, it will be cold and wet. We should take waterproof jackets and warm clothes. Friend 1: At least the hotel has an indoor pool, and we can move the picnic to another day if the rain continues. After making the decision, the remaining arrangements were straightforward.

<!-- word_comment_id: 155; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 156. Two friends are talking about their trip. What will the weather be like? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set013-q001 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"people_relationships","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"156"} -->
> **157. Jana is talking to her friend. What does Jana’s sister look like?**
>
> A. Blonde
>
> B. Tall
>
> <u>C. Short</u>
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set013-q001-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 156 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Jana: My sister is not blonde; she has dark brown hair like mine. People often expect her to be tall because everyone else in our family is, but she is short. She is only a little over one metre fifty. That makes us easy to tell apart in photographs, although our faces and voices are similar enough to confuse people on the phone. People focusing only on the first detail could easily misunderstand the speaker.

<!-- word_comment_id: 156; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 157. Jana is talking to her friend. What does Jana’s sister look like? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set013-q002 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"time_date_duration","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"157"} -->
> **158. A man is talking about his eating habit. What time does he usually eat?**
>
> A. 8 o'clock
>
> <u>B. 7 o'clock</u>
>
> C. 5 o'clock
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set013-q002-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 157 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Man: I sometimes eat at eight if I work late, and five o'clock is when I usually have a small snack. My normal evening meal is at seven o'clock. That gives me time to cook after work without eating too close to bedtime. At weekends, the schedule is less regular because I may meet friends or have a larger lunch with my family. There was still enough time to arrive, prepare and deal with any small delay.

<!-- word_comment_id: 157; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 158. A man is talking about his eating habit. What time does he usually eat? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set013-q003 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"activities_events_details","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"158"} -->
> **159. A professor is talking to his student. What does the professor ask his student to do?**
>
> A. Submit a report
>
> <u>B. Speak at the conference</u>
>
> C. Write an essay
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set013-q003-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 158 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Professor: Your report is progressing well, and I do not want another essay from you this term. I would like you to speak at the conference next month. Your research would be valuable to the audience, and the presentation only needs to last ten minutes. Start with the main result, use a few clear slides and leave time for at the end. Nothing else needed to change once the main decision had been made.

<!-- word_comment_id: 158; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 159. A professor is talking to his student. What does the professor ask his student to do? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set013-q004 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"activities_events_details","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"159"} -->
> **160. A man and a woman are talking about their old school days. What was the man's favorite thing about school?**
>
> A. Math classes
>
> <u>B. History classes</u>
>
> C. Sports
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set013-q004-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 159 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Woman: Was sport your favourite part of school? Man: I enjoyed football, but I was never especially good at it. Maths lessons were useful, although I found them stressful. My favourite thing was history classes. Our teacher told stories rather than simply giving us dates, and the class often discussed why people made particular decisions. That subject first made me interested in reading non-fiction. The later details made the distinction much clearer than the first impression.

<!-- word_comment_id: 159; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 160. A man and a woman are talking about their old school days. What was the man's favorite thing about school? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set013-q005 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"reasons_opinions_purpose","classification_status":"review_required","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"160"} -->
> **161. Listen to a radio man talking about a new popular song. Which is the most attractive part of the song?**
>
> A. The melody
>
> B. The rhythm
>
> <u>C. The words</u>
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set013-q005-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 160 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Radio presenter: The melody of the new song is pleasant, and the rhythm makes it easy to dance to. The most attractive part, however, is the words. They tell a simple but emotional story that many listeners can recognise. The singer delivers them clearly, and several lines stay in your mind long after the music has finished. That is probably why the song became popular so

<!-- word_comment_id: 160; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 161. Listen to a radio man talking about a new popular song. Which is the most attractive part of the song? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set013-q006 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"reasons_opinions_purpose","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"161"} -->
> **162. A man is talking about the environment of the countryside. What is the main cause of poor air quality?**
>
> A. Factories
>
> B. Cars
>
> <u>C. Fires from the countryside</u>
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set013-q006-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 161 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Man: There are few factories in this area, and traffic is much lighter than in the city. The main cause of poor air is fires in the countryside. During dry periods, farmers and landowners sometimes burn vegetation, and the smoke spreads across nearby villages. On still days it can remain in the air for hours, so residents are advised to keep windows closed. That detail changed how everyone involved understood the problem.

<!-- word_comment_id: 161; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 162. A man is talking about the environment of the countryside. What is the main cause of poor air quality? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set013-q007 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"numbers_money_codes","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"162"} -->
> **163. Stephanie is looking for a job taking care of children. How old is she?**
>
> <u>A. 21</u>
>
> B. 25
>
> C. 30
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set013-q007-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 162 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Stephanie: I am applying for the childcare position. The advertisement says applicants must be over twenty, and I know some parents assumed I was twenty-five or even thirty because of my previous experience. I am twenty-one. I have completed a childcare course, worked at a summer camp and regularly looked after two younger cousins. I can also provide references from both families. The comparison became easier once the less important details were separated.

<!-- word_comment_id: 162; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 163. Stephanie is looking for a job taking care of children. How old is she? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set013-q008 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"activities_events_details","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"163"} -->
> **164. A man and woman are discussing their plans for the evening. What do the man and woman decide to do in the evening?**
>
> A. Go to the movies
>
> <u>B. Make plans later</u>
>
> C. Stay at home
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set013-q008-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 163 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Man: We could go to the movies, but neither of us has checked what is showing. Woman: Staying at home is another option, although the weather may improve later. Man: Let's make plans later, after we finish work and know how tired we feel. Woman: Agreed. I'll call around six, and then we can decide whether to go out or cook something at home. After making the decision, the remaining arrangements were straightforward.

<!-- word_comment_id: 163; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 164. A man and woman are discussing their plans for the evening. What do the man and woman decide to do in the evening? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set013-q009 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"places_directions","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"164"} -->
> **165. A woman is talking about her holiday plan. Where is she going on holidays?**
>
> A. The beach
>
> <u>B. The mountains</u>
>
> C. The city
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set013-q009-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 164 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Woman: I considered a holiday at the beach because I enjoy swimming, and a city break would offer plenty of museums and restaurants. This year I'm going to the mountains. I have booked a small cabin near several walking routes. I want fresh air, evenings and a chance to spend time outdoors without the noise and crowds I experience at home. The nearby landmarks were useful, although neither was the destination itself.

<!-- word_comment_id: 164; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 165. A woman is talking about her holiday plan. Where is she going on holidays? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set013-q010 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"places_directions","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"165"} -->
> **166. A tour guide is talking about the group's traveling schedule. Where will the group wait for the bus?**
>
> <u>A. By the hotel's main entrance</u>
>
> B. In the lobby
>
> C. At the train station
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set013-q010-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 165 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Tour guide: The group should not wait in the hotel lobby because the driver cannot see us there. The train station is part of tomorrow's journey, not today's. We will wait by the hotel's main entrance. Please be there ten minutes before departure, keep your luggage together and tell me immediately if someone from your room has not arrived. Anyone following the directions carefully should reach the correct place without difficulty.

<!-- word_comment_id: 165; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 166. A tour guide is talking about the group's traveling schedule. Where will the group wait for the bus? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set013-q011 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"places_directions","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"166"} -->
> **167. Listen to a tour guide introducing the tour. Where will tea be served?**
>
> A. In the park
>
> B. The hotel
>
> <u>C. The river boat</u>
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set013-q011-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 166 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">

Tour guide: We will walk through the park in the morning and return to the hotel after the evening meal. Tea will be served on the river boat during the afternoon. Tables will already be prepared when we board, and you can enjoy the city view while the boat moves slowly downstream. Please tell staff in advance if you need a vegetarian snack. The final location also allowed the group to continue the journey together.

<!-- word_comment_id: 166; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 167. Listen to a tour guide introducing the tour. Where will tea be served? -->

Alice: I have already tried several times, but nobody has answered yet.

A second check confirmed that no part of the total had been counted twice.

Anyone planning outdoor activities should still take a jacket because conditions may become cooler during the evening. The chosen place was more convenient and easier for everyone to find.

The clarification prevented the same misunderstanding from affecting the next decision.

The shop is opposite the gift shop, beside a small pharmacy. Friend: Ah, I think I know it! Man: I'll wait at a table near the window. If you come from the station, cross at the traffic lights and you should see it immediately.

> A. His aunt and uncle
>
> <u>B. His sister and her children</u>
>
> C. His cousin
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set014-q008-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 176 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Tom: Hi Mum, I wanted to update you about this weekend. Mother: Of course! Are your aunt and uncle still coming? Tom: No, they're not coming, and my cousin has exams. My sister and her children are visiting instead. Mother: Oh how lovely! When do they arrive? Tom: They will arrive on Saturday morning and stay for two nights. I have prepared the spare room, but I still need to find some games for the children and buy enough food for everyone. Mother: That's wonderful, they'll love it.

<!-- word_comment_id: 176; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 177. Tom is calling his mom. Who is visiting Tom this weekend? -->

>
> B. Become famous
>
> C. Earn money
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set014-q009-audio -->

<!-- word_comment_ids: 177 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Woman: I do not expect writing to make me famous, and I know it may take a long time to earn much money. I want to become a writer because I hope to help people. Stories can make readers feel understood, introduce them to different experiences and explain difficult subjects in a personal way. I am especially interested in writing for teenagers who feel isolated or uncertain.

<!-- word_comment_id: 177; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 178. A woman shares her job with her friend. Why does she want to become a writer? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set014-q010 -->

>
> <u>B. Water</u>
>
> C. Coffee
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set014-q010-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 178 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Brother: Lucy, I just finished my run. Is there anything to drink? Lucy: There is tea in the cupboard and coffee beside the kettle, but you said you wanted something simple after your run. Have some water. Brother: That sounds good actually. Lucy: I put a bottle in the fridge, and there are clean glasses on the table. Drink that first, then decide whether you still want anything else. You have not had enough to drink today. Brother: You're right, thanks. I'll have the water.

<!-- word_comment_id: 178; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 179. Lucy is calling her brother. What does the brother have to drink? -->

Lucy: My sister is not simply taller than me, and we are certainly not completely different. We have similar characters. We are both calm in difficult situations, enjoy planning things carefully and need time alone after a busy day. Our appearance and hobbies are not identical, but friends often say our reactions and sense of humour are surprisingly alike. The full description explained why the earlier possibilities had sounded convincing.

> **182. A tour guide is talking about birds' behaviors. What do birds do in the winter?**
>
> <u>A. They stay together in groups for protection</u>
>
> B. They fly south
>
> C. They hunt for food
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set014-q013-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 181 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Tour guide: Some birds fly south in winter, and all species must continue searching for food. The birds we are observing behave differently: they stay together in groups for protection. A large group makes it easier to notice danger and helps them conserve warmth during cold nights. You may see several hundred gathering in the trees shortly before the sun goes down. That option matched the person's needs more closely than the alternatives.

<!-- word_comment_id: 181; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 182. A tour guide is talking about birds' behaviors. What do birds do in the winter? -->

</details>

The entrance has a large blue sign. Training starts at six, so arrive early because the changing rooms can become crowded. The final location also allowed the group to continue the journey together.

The choice fitted the situation better than the other ideas they had discussed.

>
> B. 5pm
>
> <u>C. 1pm</u>
>

<!-- word_comment_ids: 187 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">

Our match starts at one p.m. Friend: Oh, that's much earlier than I thought! Jorge: Yes. We should arrive by half past twelve to find seats and buy food. I'll meet you outside the main gate near the ticket office. Friend: Perfect, I'll be there by twelve.

>

>

<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 188 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">

Man: The sports building at my school is green, and the science block was painted blue last summer. The teachers' building is white. It stands near the main entrance and has a clock above the doors. New students often use it as a landmark because it can be seen from almost every part of the central playground. People focusing only on the first detail could easily misunderstand the speaker.

<!-- word_comment_id: 188; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 189. A man is describing his school. What color is the teacher's building? -->

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set015-q008 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"people_relationships","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"189"} -->
> **190. James is talking about his family members. In what way does his mother and his aunt alike?**
>
> A. They had similar hair styles
>
> B. They had similar eye colors
>
> <u>C. They were thin</u>
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set015-q008-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 189 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

James: My mother and aunt did not have similar hairstyles, and their eye colours were different. What made them look alike was that they were both thin. Old family photographs show this clearly, even though they wore very different clothes. As they grew older, their faces remained similar, but my aunt became much shorter than my mother. The most important feature appeared before the speaker moved on to other information.

<!-- word_comment_id: 189; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 190. James is talking about his family members. In what way does his mother and his aunt alike? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set015-q009 -->

>
> A. Go to the beach and swim
>
> <u>B. Go to theater and play sports</u>
>
> C. Watch TV and read books
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set015-q009-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 190 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Sarah: I sometimes watch television and read when I want a evening. In my free time, though, I usually go to the theatre and play sports. I attend a live performance about once a month and play tennis at weekends. I enjoy the variety: the theatre gives me new ideas, while sport keeps me active and helps me meet people. Nothing else needed to change once the main decision had been made.

<!-- word_comment_id: 190; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 191. Listen to Sarah talking about her hobby. What does she do in her free time? -->

</details>

>
> C. Go swimming

Tour guide: Museum visits are arranged for the morning, and swimming is available before breakfast or after dinner. In the afternoon, guests can play golf. Equipment can be hired at the course, and beginners may book a short lesson. Anyone who does not want to play can relax at the hotel or join a guided walk through the nearby village. The choice fitted the situation better than the other ideas they had discussed.

<!-- word_comment_id: 191; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 192. A tour guide is talking about the vacation list of activities. What can people do in the afternoon? -->

<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 192 -->

Please arrange them in four rows of five and leave a clear path to the door. The tables can remain at the back for documents and drinks. No extra charge or item needed to be added to that figure.

<!-- word_comment_id: 192; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 193. A woman is discussing with her team. How many chairs does she need? -->

>
> B. Print the documents
>
> C. Arrange the seating
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set015-q012-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 193 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Teacher: I printed the documents yesterday, and the caretaker is arranging the seating now. My current task is ordering the food for the meeting. I need to confirm vegetarian choices and the final number of guests before midday. The caterer also needs to know what time to deliver everything. After that, I'll check the presentation equipment and place copies of the agenda on each table.

<!-- word_comment_id: 193; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 194. Listen to a teacher talking about meeting preparations. What is the teacher preparing for the meeting now? -->

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set015-q013 -->

>
> <u>A. Friday</u>
>
> B. Thursday
>
> C. Tuesday
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set015-q013-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 194 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Adam: Hi, could I borrow your computer this week? Friend: Sure, when exactly do you need it? Adam: I first thought I would need it on Tuesday, but the research session was moved. Thursday is when I collect the data. I will need the computer on Friday to analyse the results and prepare the report. Friend: No problem, Friday works for me. Adam: Great. I can return it on Saturday morning, and I will save all my work in a separate folder. Friend: Take your time, no rush.

<!-- word_comment_id: 194; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 195. Adam is calling his friend. When will he need the computer? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set016-q001 -->

After making the decision, the remaining arrangements were straightforward.

<!-- word_comment_id: 195; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 196. Listen to an actor discussing his hobbies. What does the actor like to do in his free time? -->

The other possibilities were useful to discuss but were not selected.

>

>
> C. He enjoys the weather

That option matched the person's needs more closely than the alternatives.

<!-- word_comment_id: 197; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 198. Listening to a man talking about his business trip. What does he like in Dubai? -->

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set016-q004 -->

>

Nothing else needed to change once the main decision had been made.

<!-- word_comment_id: 198; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 199. Listen to a conversation between the teacher and a parent. What will the father do? -->

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set016-q005 -->

>

>

<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->

<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Man: I considered driving to the city, but parking is expensive and the roads will be busy. The train is fast, although the station is far from my hotel. I'm going by bus. It stops near my accommodation, allows one large bag and costs less than the train. I have booked a morning service so I will arrive before lunch. The choice fitted the situation better than the other ideas they had discussed.

<!-- word_comment_id: 199; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 200. A man is talking about his holiday. How is he going to travel to the city? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set016-q006 -->

>
> C. Watches TV
>

<!-- word_comment_ids: 200 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Lily: I sometimes do yoga before breakfast, and I watch television if I am very tired. In the evening, I usually go for a walk. I follow a route through the park for about thirty minutes. It helps me relax after work and gives me some fresh air. When the weather is bad, I walk around the shopping centre instead. It also left enough time and energy for the rest of the day.

<!-- word_comment_id: 200; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 201. Lily is talking about her daily routine. What does she do in the evening? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set016-q007 -->

>

>
> <u>B. \$20</u>
>
> C. \$30
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set016-q007-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 201 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Clerk: A one-day ticket is ten dollars, while thirty dollars buys a card that includes several regional services. The standard bus card costs twenty dollars. You can use it on all city buses for one week and add more credit later. Keep the receipt until you test the card, because the office can replace it immediately if it does not work. The final record was saved so the same confusion would not happen again.

<!-- word_comment_id: 201; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 202. How much does a bus card cost? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set016-q008 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"numbers_money_codes","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"202"} -->

>

>
> <u>B. 10 pounds</u>
>
> C. 30 pounds
>

<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 202 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Woman: I thought there were twenty pounds in the kitchen drawer, but part of that money was used for groceries. Thirty pounds was the amount before we paid the delivery driver. There are ten pounds left now. I put the note in an envelope beside the shopping list, so please do not use it unless you need something essential for dinner. A second check confirmed that no part of the total had been counted twice.

<!-- word_comment_id: 202; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 203. How much money is left in the kitchen? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set016-q009 -->

Boy: Route ten goes through the town centre, and bus nine stops near the sports hall. The bus that stops closest to my home is number eight. The written information was updated immediately to prevent another mistake later.

<!-- word_comment_id: 203; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 204. What bus number stops near the boy's home? -->

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set016-q010 -->

>

>
> C. 9:30 pm
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set016-q010-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 204 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Woman: Dinner was originally planned for 8:30, but everyone will be hungry before then. Nine thirty is actually when the film we are watching should finish tomorrow, not tonight's meal time. Dinner is at 7:30 p.m. The food is nearly ready, and I only need to make the salad. Please arrive a few minutes early and help set the table. The remaining activities could continue normally once the correct time was clear.

<!-- word_comment_id: 204; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 205. What time is dinner? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set016-q011 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"time_date_duration","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"205"} -->

>
> A. 3:30 pm
>
> B. 4:30 pm
>
> <u>C. 5:30 pm</u>
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set016-q011-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 205 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Colleague 1: Could we meet at 3:30? Colleague 2: I will still be with a client then. Four thirty may also be difficult because the manager is travelling back from another office. Let's arrange the meeting for 5:30 p.m. Colleague 1: That works. I'll send the agenda now, and we can keep the discussion short enough for everyone to leave before seven.

<!-- word_comment_id: 205; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 206. What time do they arrange the meeting? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set016-q012 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"time_date_duration","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"206"} -->

>
> A. 5 o'clock
>
> B. 4 o'clock
>
> <u>C. 3 o'clock</u>
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set016-q012-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 206 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Host: The invitation originally said five o'clock, but that was the time we expected dinner to be served. Four was suggested while we were planning the decorations. The party starts at three o'clock. Guests can arrive from then, and the garden games will begin at half past three. Please use the side entrance because the front room is being prepared for food. The updated schedule suited the people involved better than either earlier suggestion.

<!-- word_comment_id: 206; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 207. What time does the party start? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set016-q013 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"time_date_duration","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"207"} -->

>
> <u>A. 3 days</u>
>
> B. 4 days
>
> C. 5 days
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set016-q013-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 207 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Man: Most full-time staff work five days, and my first offer mentioned four. The contract I accepted requires me to work three days each week. The shifts are longer, but I have four days free for study and family responsibilities. I usually work Monday, Wednesday and Friday, although the manager may occasionally change one day when the shop is especially busy. The revised timing left enough room for travel and preparation beforehand.

<!-- word_comment_id: 207; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 208. A man has a new job. How many days does the man have to work? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set017-q001 -->

>

>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set017-q001-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 208 -->

<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Manager: Daniel is not ill, and he has already eaten, so neither of those explains why he cannot do the job today. He has another appointment that was arranged several weeks ago. He told us about it in advance and will return tomorrow. We have moved the urgent task to another employee, while the less important work can wait until he comes back. The other explanations sounded possible at first but did not match the evidence.

<!-- word_comment_id: 208; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 209. Why can't he do the job today? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set017-q002 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"activities_events_details","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"209"} -->

>
> C. Get something to drink

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set017-q002-audio -->

<!-- word_comment_ids: 209 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Man: I am tired, but I am not going to sleep yet. I have had plenty to drink during the journey, so I do not need another drink. I'm going to get something to eat. I missed lunch, and there is a café beside the station that serves food until late. Afterwards, I'll continue home and try to have an early night. The alternatives remained possible on another occasion, but not this time.

<!-- word_comment_id: 209; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 210. What is the man going to do? -->

> C. food
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set017-q007-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 214 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Woman: I have packed food for the journey, and the hotel already provides books in the children's room. I'm going to take games with me. They will help everyone pass the time if the weather is bad or the train is delayed. I have chosen several card activities and one small board set, all of which fit easily into my bag. Nothing else needed to change once the main decision had been made.

<!-- word_comment_id: 214; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 215. What is she going to take with her? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set017-q008 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"activities_events_details","classification_status":"review_required","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"215"} -->
> **216. What do they decide to write about?**
>
> A. politic
>
> <u>B. historian</u>
>
> C. economy
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set017-q008-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 215 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Student 1: We could write about politics, but the subject is too broad. The economy is interesting, although another group has already chosen it. Let's write about a historian, or more clearly, an important historical figure. Student 2: Good idea. We can compare several sources, explain the person's influence and divide the research so each of us covers a different period. The choice fitted the situation better than the other ideas they had discussed.

<!-- word_comment_id: 215; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 216. What do they decide to write about? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set017-q009 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"activities_events_details","classification_status":"review_required","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"216"} -->
> **217. What will the student write about?**
>
> <u>A. land</u>
>
> B. bikes
>
> C. animals
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set017-q009-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 216 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Student: I considered writing about animals because there is plenty of information, and bikes would make a practical local topic. My project will focus on land. I want to examine how an unused area near the river could be developed without damaging the environment. I'll include maps, photographs and opinions from residents who live close to the site. It also left enough time and energy for the rest of the day.

<!-- word_comment_id: 216; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 217. What will the student write about? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set017-q010 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"objects_descriptions","classification_status":"review_required","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"217"} -->
> **218. What does the teacher want the student to do?**
>
> <u>A. Complete the assignments on time</u>
>
> B. Rest
>
> C. Study harder
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set017-q010-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 217 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Teacher: You work hard, so I am not simply asking you to study more, and you do not need a long rest from the course. What I want is for you to complete the assignments on time. Late work makes it difficult to give useful feedback before the next task. Plan smaller weekly deadlines and speak to me early if a genuine problem occurs. The alternatives remained possible on another occasion, but not this time.

<!-- word_comment_id: 217; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 218. What does the teacher want the student to do? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set017-q011 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"activities_events_details","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"218"} -->
> **219. What do the students need to do every Tuesday?**
>
> A. write a note
>
> <u>B. write a report</u>
>
> C. go camping
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set017-q011-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 218 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Teacher: Every Tuesday, students do not need to go camping, and a short note is not enough for this course. They must write a report. It should summarise the week's experiment, explain the results and mention any problems with the method. The report is due before the next lesson, so keep accurate notes while you work rather than trying to remember everything later. The practical details made that option more suitable than either earlier suggestion.

<!-- word_comment_id: 218; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 219. What do the students need to do every Tuesday? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set017-q012 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"reasons_opinions_purpose","classification_status":"review_required","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"219"} -->
> **220. Listen to the radio program about a new restaurant. What does the man complain about?**
>
> <u>A. the place was cold</u>
>
> B. the place was hot
>
> C. the place was ugly
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set017-q012-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 219 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Radio caller: The restaurant looked attractive, so I would not describe it as ugly, and it certainly was not too hot. My complaint is that the place was cold. The heating did not seem to work, and several customers kept their coats on during dinner. The food was good, but it was difficult to enjoy the meal while sitting in such an uncomfortable room. Understanding the real cause made it easier to decide what to do next.

<!-- word_comment_id: 219; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 220. Listen to the radio program about a new restaurant. What does the man complain about? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set017-q013 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"people_relationships","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"220"} -->
> **221. Who does she live with? / A woman is talking about her vacation. What is the relationship between Lisa and the speaker?**
>
> <u>A. Best friends</u>
>
> B. Cousins
>
> C. Sisters
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set017-q013-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 220 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Woman: Lisa and I are not cousins or sisters, although people sometimes assume we are related because we travel and live together. We are best friends. We met at school and have shared a flat for the past two years. We understand each other's habits well, divide the household jobs fairly and often spend holidays together when our work schedules allow it. Knowing how long they had known each other removed the earlier confusion.

<!-- word_comment_id: 220; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 221. Who does she live with? / A woman is talking about her vacation. What is the relationship between Lisa and the speaker? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set018-q001 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"objects_descriptions","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"221"} -->
> **222. A man is talking about his jobs. What does the man want to do next?**
>
> A. Become a history teacher
>
> <u>B. Become a writer</u>
>
> C. Become a chef
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set018-q001-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 221 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Man: I worked as a chef when I was younger and later taught history. I do not want to return to either job full-time. What I want to do next is become a writer. I have several story ideas and have started planning a novel. I'll continue teaching part-time at first so I can earn money while developing a regular writing routine. That option matched the person's needs more closely than the alternatives.

<!-- word_comment_id: 221; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 222. A man is talking about his jobs. What does the man want to do next? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set018-q002 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"reasons_opinions_purpose","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"222"} -->
> **223. Why does she like the manager?**
>
> A. He was always strict
>
> <u>B. He taught her a lot</u>
>
> C. He was very friendly
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set018-q002-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 222 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Woman: My manager was not always friendly, and sometimes he was strict because the work required high standards. I liked him because he taught me a lot. He explained how to handle difficult clients, organise priorities and correct mistakes without becoming defensive. The responsibilities he gave me helped me develop confidence and prepared me for the position I have now. The clarification prevented the same misunderstanding from affecting the next decision.

<!-- word_comment_id: 222; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 223. Why does she like the manager? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set018-q003 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"activities_events_details","classification_status":"review_required","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"223"} -->
> **224. A woman is talking about her job. How is being a writer different from other jobs?**
>
> A. She works 9 to 5
>
> <u>B. She works at irregular times</u>
>
> C. She works at a desk all day
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set018-q003-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 223 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Woman: Many office employees work from nine to five and remain at a desk for most of the day. Being a writer is different because I work at irregular times. Some mornings I start before sunrise, while on other days I write late at night. The schedule depends on deadlines and when I can concentrate, although I still try to keep several hours free for family life.

<!-- word_comment_id: 223; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 224. A woman is talking about her job. How is being a writer different from other jobs? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set018-q004 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"numbers_money_codes","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"224"} -->

>
> A. Two
>
> <u>B. One</u>
>
> C. Three
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set018-q004-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 224 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Receptionist: Today's list contains two clients from France and three from the UK, which may be why the numbers became confused. There is one American client. Her name is Leslie, and her appointment is at eleven. Please place the correct form on the reception desk and confirm her contact number when she arrives, as the previous record contains an old address. No extra charge or item needed to be added to that figure.

<!-- word_comment_id: 224; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 225. A receptionist is checking the client list of a clinic. How many American clients are there? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set018-q005 -->

>

>
> <u>C. In the corner</u>
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set018-q005-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->

<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Girl: Hi, I think I left my bag in your café this afternoon. I paid at the counter, but I definitely had it after that, and I did not sit near the door. Could you look in the corner beside the tall plant? That is where my table was. The bag is small and brown, with a set of keys and a notebook inside. They planned to arrive early enough to avoid any further confusion there.

<!-- word_comment_id: 225; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 226. Listen to a girl calling the cafe. Where did she ask the coffee shop to look for her lost item? -->

>
> B. Wednesday
>
> <u>C. Monday</u>
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set018-q006-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 226 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

The school has finally confirmed the date for the parent-teacher meeting. We originally planned to hold it on Friday, but several teachers will be attending a training course later in the week. Wednesday was also considered, although the main hall is already booked for a school performance. So the meeting will take place on Monday. Parents should arrive about ten minutes before their appointment.

<!-- word_comment_id: 226; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 227. When will the parent-teacher meeting take place? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set018-q007 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"numbers_money_codes","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"227"} -->
> **228. How much does the standard city bus card cost?**
>
> A. \$25
>
> B. \$15
>
> <u>C. \$20</u>
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set018-q007-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 227 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

If you're planning to use public transport regularly, the monthly bus card is probably the best option. The airport card costs twenty-five dollars, while the student card is only fifteen. For most passengers, though, the standard city card costs twenty dollars and allows unlimited journeys within the city centre.

<!-- word_comment_id: 227; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 228. How much does the standard city bus card cost? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set018-q008 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"time_date_duration","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"228"} -->

Hello, I'm calling to arrange our lunch meeting. I'd like to confirm the time with you. I'm available at 1:30 p.m., so I think that would be a good time for us to meet. Please let me know if it works for you. Give me a call back when you have a chance.

<!-- word_comment_id: 228; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 229. What time will they meet for lunch? -->

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set018-q009 -->

I'm really happy that we've been invited to the party. It's going to be held next week at my friend's house. I think it'll be a lot of fun, and we can spend some time together and meet new people. I'll bring some games so everyone can have fun. I think the games will make the party more exciting.

<!-- word_comment_id: 229; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 230. What will the speaker bring to the party? -->

Hi, I'm calling to update you about the store. As you know, the store is closed this week because we're having some repairs done. Woman: I'm looking for a new pair of shoes. I like the blue ones, but they're a little unusual. The black ones are nice, but they're boring. Shop Assistant:

<!-- word_comment_id: 231; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 232. Which shoes will the woman buy? -->

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set018-q012 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"activities_events_details","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"232"} -->

>

>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set018-q012-audio -->

Woman: Oh, then I'll have the cake. It sounds good.

>
> A. 4:30 p.m.
>
> B. 5:00 p.m.
>
> <u>C. 5:30 p.m.</u>
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set018-q013-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 233 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Hi, I'm calling about our meeting today. I'm sorry, but I need to change the time. My brother is coming back from London at 4:30 p.m., so I have to go to the station to pick him up. Could we meet at 5:30 p.m. instead? We can meet somewhere near the station. I hope that's okay with you. Please let me know.

<!-- word_comment_id: 233; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 234. What time will they meet instead? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set019-q001 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"time_date_duration","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"234"} -->
> **235. What time does the speaker decide to get up?**
>
> A. 6:00 a.m.
>
> <u>B. 7:00 a.m.</u>
>
> C. 8:00 a.m.
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set019-q001-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 234 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

I used to get up at 8 a.m., but I didn't have enough time to get ready before going to work. So, I started getting up at 6 a.m. However, I felt really tired during the day. In the end, I decided to get up at 7 a.m. instead. It gives me enough time to get ready, and I don't feel as tired. I think it's a good routine for me.

<!-- word_comment_id: 234; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 235. What time does the speaker decide to get up? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set019-q002 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"numbers_money_codes","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"235"} -->
> **236. How many bedrooms does the new house have?**
>
> A. Two bedrooms
>
> B. Three bedrooms
>
> <u>C. Four bedrooms</u>
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set019-q002-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 235 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

I used to live in a small house with my wife. It had two bedrooms, but after we had children, we moved to a three-bedroom house because we needed more space. Now, we've moved into a new house, and it's much bigger. It has four bedrooms, so there's plenty of room for everyone.

<!-- word_comment_id: 235; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 236. How many bedrooms does the new house have? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set019-q003 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"activities_events_details","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"236"} -->
> **237. What does the teacher tell the student to do?**
>
> <u>A. Submit the assignment on time</u>
>
> B. Change the assignment topic
>
> C. Ask another student for help
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set019-q003-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 236 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Teacher: Is everything going well with your assignment? Are you having any problems? Student: I'm a little worried. I don't think I can finish it until next week. I chose a topic that's difficult, and I'm having trouble finding enough information. Teacher: I understand, but you need to finish the assignment by the deadline. You must submit it on time. If you're having problems with the topic, we can discuss them, but don't miss the deadline.

<!-- word_comment_id: 236; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 237. What does the teacher tell the student to do? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set019-q004 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"reasons_opinions_purpose","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"237"} -->
> **238. Why did the woman move abroad?**
>
> <u>A. Be closer to her family</u>
>
> B. Get a higher salary
>
> C. Improve her career prospects
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set019-q004-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 237 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

There were actually some good opportunities in my hometown. The salary was high, and there were good prospects for career development. However, I decided to move abroad because my sister lives there. It means I can be closer to my sister and spend more time with her. That was the main reason I chose to work in another country.

<!-- word_comment_id: 237; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 238. Why did the woman move abroad? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set019-q005 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"reasons_opinions_purpose","classification_status":"review_required","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"238"} -->
> **239. What does the man enjoy most about business trips?**
>
> <u>A. Learn new ideas</u>
>
> B. Meet business partners
>
> C. A break from the usual daily routine
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set019-q005-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 238 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Woman: I think business trips are a nice break from our usual daily routine. Man: I agree. What I enjoy most is getting the chance to learn new ideas and see how other companies work. Woman: Yes, and we can also meet business partners and discuss new opportunities. Man: Exactly. It's a good way to learn and develop new business ideas.

<!-- word_comment_id: 238; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 239. What does the man enjoy most about business trips? -->

</details>

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set019-q006 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"numbers_money_codes","classification_status":"reviewed","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"239"} -->
> **240. How many pages of the assignment?**
>
> A. 6
>
> B. 5
>
> <u>C. 4</u>
>

<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->

<details class="audio-transcript">

Teacher: The assignment does not need to be six pages, and five would still be longer than the limit. It should be four pages, including the introduction and conclusion. The reference list may go on a separate page and will not count. Focus on analysing the evidence rather than repeating background information simply to make the work appear longer. A second check confirmed that no part of the total had been counted twice.

> **246. Listen to a man calling his friend. Why does he make the call?**
>
> A. To ask for directions
>
> B. To invite her out
>
> <u>C. To tell her he will be late</u>
>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set019-q012-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 245 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Man: Hi, I'm not calling to ask for directions, and this is not an invitation to go out. I wanted to tell you that I will be late. Friend: Oh no, what happened? Man: My train has stopped because of a signal problem, and the driver expects a delay of about twenty minutes. Friend: Don't worry, we'll wait a bit. Man: Please begin without me if everyone else arrives, and I'll come straight to the restaurant from the station. Friend: Alright, see you soon!

<!-- word_comment_id: 245; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 246. Listen to a man calling his friend. Why does he make the call? -->

>

>

>

<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Girl: Mum, could you pick me up at the airport? Mother: Of course! Are you alright? Girl: I am happy to be home, and I am certainly tired after the long flight, but the main problem is that I feel sick. I have a headache and an upset stomach, so I do not want to take the crowded bus. Mother: Oh, poor thing! I'll come right away. Girl: Thank you. I'll wait near the information desk until you arrive. Mother: Stay warm, I'm on my way.

I've decided I really need to change my morning routine. Getting up at eight means I'm always rushing and sometimes I don't even have time for breakfast. >

>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set022-q012-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 284 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

Interviewer: Why did you decide to take a job overseas? Was it because the salary was better? Woman: No, actually, the pay was almost exactly the same. Interviewer: Was it mainly because you wanted to experience another culture? Woman: That was interesting, but it wasn't the main reason. My sister had been living there for several years, and before I moved we could only see each other once or twice a year. Taking the job meant we could meet much more often. Interviewer: I can see why that appealed to you.

<!-- word_comment_id: 284; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 285. Why did the woman take a job overseas? -->

<!-- source_key: listening-p1-set022-q013 -->
<!-- listening_metadata: {"information_type":"activities_events_details","classification_status":"review_required","taxonomy_version":"listening-p1-v1","audio_script_status":"source_word_comment","word_comment_id":"285"} -->
> **286. What does the speaker like most about business trips?**
>
> A. Staying in hotels
>
> B. Taking early flights
>
> <u>C. Meeting people from different companies</u>

<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p1-set022-q013-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comment -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 285 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

People often ask me what I like most about travelling for work. It isn't the hotels - honestly, after a while they all feel the same - and I certainly don't enjoy getting up at four in the morning for an early flight. The best part is talking to people from different companies. They often solve problems in ways I haven't considered before, and I usually return to the office with completely fresh approaches that I can try in my own work.

<!-- word_comment_id: 285; author: APTIS Transcript; anchor: 286. What does the speaker like most about business trips? -->

</details>

### **Part 2: Information Matching - Question 14**

#### Topic: Protect the environment 1

<!-- group_key: listening-p2-set001 -->
<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p2-set001-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comments -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 286,287,288,289 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

**Segment 1 — Does not use commercial cleaning products**

Person A: I find the act of giving away old or secondhand items a bit hypocritical. Why do people give away their old things, only to go out and buy the same item but brand new? Not only does this fail to protect the environment, but it also causes double the harm. Personally, I have a way of protecting the environment by not releasing harmful chemicals into it. I do this by not purchasing chemical cleaning solutions. Instead, I make use of natural products like lemons to create my own floor-cleaning solutions.

<!-- word_comment_id: 286; author: Châu Phạm; anchor: Does not use commercial cleaning products -->

**Segment 2 — Does not use commercial cleaning products**

Person B: I often don't have time to think about how to reuse my belongings or clothes, but I don't want to throw them away because that would be wasteful. So, I think a better way is to donate them to charity organizations or give them to others. Many people are in need of these things, and giving away my old items also makes me feel happier because I'm helping others.

<!-- word_comment_id: 287; author: Châu Phạm; anchor: Does not use commercial cleaning products -->

**Segment 3 — Buy environmentally friendly products**

Person C: A good way to protect the environment is by not using plastic bags or by bringing reusable bags when going to the market, which helps reduce the disposal of plastic bags, a material that is difficult to decompose. However, I'm a very busy person and rarely have time to prepare my own bags when going shopping, and I usually drive to buy groceries quickly instead of walking. I guess I can't protect the environment by not driving. But I have a principle of only buying eco-friendly products, so I think I’m still contributing in a small way to protecting the Earth.

<!-- word_comment_id: 288; author: Châu Phạm; anchor: Buy environmentally friendly products -->

**Segment 4 — Reuse containers for storing food**

Person D: I see that too many people are wasting food containers, as they often throw them away after use, such as glass jars or plastic bottles. I can reuse them to store food and neatly organize them in my refrigerator. Reusing these containers is a very easy way to protect the environment that anyone can do.

<!-- word_comment_id: 289; author: Châu Phạm; anchor: Reuse containers for storing food -->

</details>

| Item | Answer / matching detail |
|---|---|
<!-- source_key: listening-p2-set001-q001 -->
| Person 1 | Does not use commercial cleaning products |
<!-- source_key: listening-p2-set001-q002 -->
| Person 2 | Does not use commercial cleaning products |
<!-- source_key: listening-p2-set001-q003 -->
| Person 3 | Buy environmentally friendly products |
<!-- source_key: listening-p2-set001-q004 -->
| Person 4 | Reuse containers for storing food |

#### Topic: Protect the environment 2

<!-- group_key: listening-p2-set002 -->
<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p2-set002-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comments -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 290,291,292,293 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

**Segment 1 — Shopping online**

A: I usually shop online to reduce the need to drive to the supermarket, which produces harmful emissions for the environment. Additionally, I can avoid using plastic bags for groceries when shopping in person. By simply sitting at home and clicking a few buttons, I can buy everything I want without emitting harmful gases from a car.

<!-- word_comment_id: 290; author: Châu Phạm; anchor: Shopping online -->

**Segment 2 — Not driving to work**

B: I used to drive to work for a long period, but then I realized that the excessive use of personal cars contributes significantly to environmental pollution. Therefore, I chose to ride a bicycle to work, which benefits both the environment and my health.

<!-- word_comment_id: 291; author: Châu Phạm; anchor: Not driving to work -->

**Segment 3 — Using less electricity**

C: I'm not that into environmental protection and usually just do what’s convenient for me. But my electric bill last month shocked me because it was so expensive, and I realized that I needed to save money. So from now on, I’ll have to manage my electricity use more efficiently.

<!-- word_comment_id: 292; author: Châu Phạm; anchor: Using less electricity -->

**Segment 4 — Using less water**

D: I think a lot of people overemphasize protecting the environment by cutting down on personal vehicles, avoiding plastic bags, planting a lot of trees, and so on. But I personally believe we should focus on protecting the environment through small daily actions we often overlook. For example, turning off the water while brushing our teeth or washing our hands. If everyone saves a little water like that, it would add up to a huge amount saved globally.

<!-- word_comment_id: 293; author: Châu Phạm; anchor: Using less water -->

</details>

|---|---|

<!-- source_key: listening-p2-set002-q002 -->

<!-- source_key: listening-p2-set002-q003 -->

<!-- source_key: listening-p2-set002-q004 -->

#### Topic: Online shopping

<!-- group_key: listening-p2-set003 -->
<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p2-set003-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comments -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 294,295,296,297 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

**Segment 1 — The products are delivered**

A: "I used to be the person who didn’t like shopping online, but ever since I experienced home delivery, I no longer shop in stores. When I shop online, the delivery person brings the package right to my door, even up to my floor, and knocks on my apartment door, so I don’t even need to step outside. I really enjoy this convenience."

<!-- word_comment_id: 294; author: Châu Phạm; anchor: The products are delivered -->

**Segment 2 — It is cheaper**

B: "Shopping online has helped me save quite a bit of money. After a few months of online shopping, I was able to save enough to buy a new bicycle. But don't get me wrong, I haven't been eating any less or cutting back on the groceries I buy. It's just that online shopping allows me to compare prices and choose the store with the best deal."

<!-- word_comment_id: 295; author: Châu Phạm; anchor: It is cheaper -->

**Segment 3 — It saves time**

C: "I’m a pretty busy person, and I often don’t have enough time because of my hectic work schedule. That’s why I always shop online to save time. This way, I don’t need to go to the store in person, and someone will deliver the items directly to me. Additionally, online shopping is very convenient because, with just a few clicks, I can buy everything I want without having to go from one store to another."

<!-- word_comment_id: 296; author: Châu Phạm; anchor: It saves time -->

**Segment 4 — There are more choices**

D: "I don't shop online because it's cheaper or because of the fast shipping. I shop online because, when browsing online stores, I have a wide range of choices. For the same item, there are many different types and brands for me to choose from and compare, which allows me to pick the one I like the most."

<!-- word_comment_id: 297; author: Châu Phạm; anchor: There are more choices -->

</details>

#### Topic: Listening to music

<!-- group_key: listening-p2-set004 -->
<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p2-set004-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comments -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 298,299,300,301 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

**Segment 1 — To relax**

A: "I see a lot of people listen to music right when they wake up to help them feel alert. But if I listen to music as soon as I wake up, it gives me a headache all day and makes it hard for me to focus on anything. So, I just listen to music to relax when I’m feeling comfortable, not for any other reason."

<!-- word_comment_id: 298; author: Châu Phạm; anchor: To relax -->

**Segment 2 — After waking up**

B: "I usually listen to a pop or rock song as soon as I get out of bed in the morning because it helps me wake up instantly. If I don't listen to music at that moment, I don't know when I'll be able to get up and go to work."

<!-- word_comment_id: 299; author: Châu Phạm; anchor: After waking up -->

**Segment 3 — While singing**

C: "My brothers and friends often say that I have a beautiful voice, so I often sing when I meet them. I usually play music on the TV and hold the remote control, pretending it's a microphone so I can sing."

<!-- word_comment_id: 300; author: Châu Phạm; anchor: While singing -->

**Segment 4 — While studying**

D: "I have a habit of listening to music while studying; it helps me stay motivated and alert while doing my assignments. If I don't play music, I could fall asleep at any moment, and I'll study in a discouraged mood."

<!-- word_comment_id: 301; author: Châu Phạm; anchor: While studying -->

</details>

#### Topic: Outdoor activities

<!-- group_key: listening-p2-set005 -->
<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p2-set005-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comments -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 302,303,304,305 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

**Segment 1 — horse riding**

A: "My hometown is in the countryside, and we have a very large and beautiful farm. I’ve been learning to ride horses since I was a child, and it’s still my hobby to this day. Whenever I want to relax or enjoy the scenery, I ride my horse along the farm and across the meadows."

<!-- word_comment_id: 302; author: Châu Phạm; anchor: horse riding -->

**Segment 2 — going for a run**

B: "My husband and I often wake up early and go to a nearby forest for exercise. We usually go jogging in the early morning to stay healthy, and we really enjoy the fresh air and natural scenery in the forest."

<!-- word_comment_id: 303; author: Châu Phạm; anchor: going for a run -->

**Segment 3 — climbing**

C: "I used to love mountain biking, enjoying the thrill of riding down steep slopes. It was really an amazing and adventurous feeling. However, after an injury from a fall, I became haunted by the fear of crashing and now I'm scared of this sport. But I still love the mountains, so I’ve decided to just go hiking every weekend instead."

<!-- word_comment_id: 304; author: Châu Phạm; anchor: climbing -->

**Segment 4 — mountain biking**

D: "My father often encourages me to ride horses because it’s a sport he enjoys. But I’m very afraid of this animal and never want to learn how to ride, even though there’s a riding school near my house. Instead, I prefer mountain biking with my friends to conquer high peaks."

<!-- word_comment_id: 305; author: Châu Phạm; anchor: mountain biking -->

#### Topic: The place to run

<!-- group_key: listening-p2-set006 -->
<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p2-set006-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comments -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 306,307,308,309 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

**Segment 1 — in the fitness centre**

A: "My friends often invite me to go jogging on the street, but I find it too dangerous. There’s a lot of traffic, which can be dangerous for people jogging on the street. So, I bought a one-year gym membership and go there to exercise every day."

<!-- word_comment_id: 306; author: Châu Phạm; anchor: in the fitness centre -->

**Segment 2 — at the seaside**

B: "I see many people wake up early to go jogging and exercise to stay healthy. As for me, I usually walk along the beach simply to relax because there’s a beautiful beach near my house. Every morning, I spend time walking on the white sand; it really helps a lot with my mental well-being."

<!-- word_comment_id: 307; author: Châu Phạm; anchor: at the seaside -->

**Segment 3 — in the street**

C: "I usually wake up early in the morning to exercise, and I choose to jog on the street because of its convenience. I just need to step out of the house, and I can start exercising. However, I have to go jogging early to avoid cyclists. My friends go to the gym to exercise a lot, but I find something artificial about the whole thing."

<!-- word_comment_id: 308; author: Châu Phạm; anchor: in the street -->

**Segment 4 — on the running track**

D: "Near my house, there’s a stadium where many people go to exercise. I often run on the track there, and it makes me feel like I’m running more professionally. My personality is quite competitive, so I also time myself and race with my friends there."

<!-- word_comment_id: 309; author: Châu Phạm; anchor: on the running track -->

#### Topic: Do exercise

<!-- group_key: listening-p2-set007 -->
<!-- audio_source_key: listening-p2-set007-audio -->
<!-- transcript_status: source_word_comments -->
<!-- word_comment_ids: 310,311,312,313 -->
<details class="audio-transcript">
<summary>Audio transcript</summary>

I feel uncomfortable as soon as I put on workout clothes. I don’t think exercise is something that suits me."

<!-- word_comment_id: 310; author: Châu Phạm; anchor: hate exercising -->

**Segment 2 — help improve work performance**

B: "I know that working out every day is good for my health and helps me get in shape. But what I've found is that exercising actually boosts my productivity a lot and really helps me focus better. So, I always try to squeeze in some exercise before heading to work—it definitely makes me more productive."

<!-- word_comment_id: 311; author: Châu Phạm; anchor: help improve work performance -->

**Segment 3 — find exercise tiring**

C: "We’ve all heard about the many benefits of exercising, and I know about them too. My friends often invite me to work out, but after I exercise, I end up feeling extremely exhausted and spend the rest of the day just lying around to recover."

<!-- word_comment_id: 312; author: Châu Phạm; anchor: find exercise tiring -->

**Segment 4 — has fun when exercising with friends**

D: "I often go to the gym to work out, but what really motivates me to workout is catching up with my friends there, not just for improving my health. Plus, it’s a great way to meet and make new friends. For me, exercising is just another chance to socialize."

<!-- word_comment_id: 313; author: Châu Phạm; anchor: has fun when exercising with friends -->
